Result: 移除每个混杂因素都使效应更大；分歧在贪心解码下仍存在于每个单元且随温度升高保持平坦。经自身可复现性归一化后，四个前沿模型在贪心解码下收敛，各保持71-73%的跨语言动作策略一致性，模型身份仅解释5.7%方差。低于约10B参数时一致性崩塌，小模型的排序主要是偶然性下限的伪影。智能体将非英语任务路由至英语，且模型在被告知后也不会放弃该行为。一个轨迹提取正则表达式而非模型本身可制造多语言失败：两个工作示例将某模型测量准确率提高26倍。

Conclusion: 跨语言动作策略分歧是工具使用智能体的结构性属性，而非测量噪声或模型身份差异所致。英语枢轴机制在因果上承担了跨语言一致性的功能，小模型的不一致表现很大程度上是测量伪影。动作策略应成为多语言评估的标准测量对象，且需要严谨的混杂控制方法。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [172] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASMI（注意力子网络互信息）作为无需训练的估计器，通过遮蔽注意力头并测量子网络间的BALD互信息来捕捉模型预测的"脆性"，从而在与置信度互补的维度上提供错误预测信号，在基于上下文的QA任务上显著提升置信度过滤器的性能，同时能自主预测其适用域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计主要依赖输出分布的宽度（即置信度或熵），但忽略了"自信但脆弱"的预测——即模型输出看似自信但在扰动其注意力路径时容易翻转的预测。这类预测往往是错误的但被单一置信度信号所掩盖，需要引入一种能补充初始置信度的不确定性信号。

Method: ASMI方法通过将模型视为由注意力头部构成的子网络集合，遮蔽不同的注意力头形成若干子网络，然后计算这些子网络预测之间的BALD互信息作为不确定性度量。为避免表面形式不同导致的虚假分歧，引入语义一致性核函数。另有Sem-ASMI变体，仅从单次贪婪解码读取信号，无需随机生成样本。还包括自适应变体，复用基线方法已抽取的采样。

Result: 在grounded QA的任务中，展示ASMI信号并非输出置信度的重复表达，而是增加了超出单次置信度和熵的错误预测信息，集中在"自信但脆弱"的预测上，在该区域可将置信度过滤器的保留误差减少约一半。在12种grounded基准-骨干组合中，Sem-ASMI在10种上达到或超过语义熵；最佳ASMI变体在8种中达到或领先最强基线，其中3种在配对检验下显著。在参数化QA上所有变体如预期回落至零成本的MSP基线，且估计在重运行中近乎确定性。

Conclusion: 注意力头的脆性与错误耦合与否才是区分适用域的关键信号。ASMI的差异性优势呈现"模态分级"特征，能预测自身适用域：在答案通过提供的上下文路由时表现强劲，在从参数化知识回忆时受到设计性限制。ASMI提供了一种无须训练、可自我判定适用范围的不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [173] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多语言环境下的安全对齐问题，发现其在四种非洲低资源语言（Twi、豪萨语、阿姆哈拉语和斯瓦希里语）中的跨语言安全迁移严重不足，有害提示仅保留不到10%的英语拒绝信号，表明当前多语言安全对齐是表面的而非深层的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐主要在英语中开发，假设其安全防护能跨语言泛化，但这一假设在低资源语言中未被充分验证，可能存在严重漏洞。

Method: 1) 构建LoDNA安全数据集，包含字面翻译和文化本地化提示；2) 提出潜在几何框架，探测隐藏状态中的拒绝表示（refusal representations），超越传统的基于生成的评估方法；3) 在Twi、豪萨语、阿姆哈拉语和斯瓦希里语四种非洲语言上进行跨语言安全迁移分析。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示在大多数语言-模型组合中保留的英语拒绝信号不足10%；字面和本地化提示在语义上高度对齐（余弦相似度0.95-0.996），但跨层漂移，表明模型编码了概念却未将其路由到安全机制；当前多语言安全对齐是表面化的。

Conclusion: 研究结果反驳了存在通用、语言无关的"危害流形"（harm manifold）的假设，证明在所研究的低资源语言中，多语言安全对齐是肤浅的，需要更深入的多语言安全训练策略。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对自2021年以来与ACL系列会议联合举办的TrustNLP研讨会共六届、144篇论文进行了系统综述，从六个信任维度（基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust框架）分类分析，发现可信NLP领域正从静态模型的"事后解释"转向生成式系统的"机制理解与主动控制"，揭示了真实性、公平性、可解释性等维度的演化轨迹及与模型能力涌现的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和生成式AI的迅速普及，可信NLP研究的重要性日益凸显。TrustNLP研讨会六年间从8篇论文增长到41篇，反映了该领域规模的显著扩张和方向的重大转变。然而，目前缺乏对这一演进过程的系统性梳理与量化分析，研究者需要一份综合性的综述来理解该领域的发展脉络、主题分布与未来方向。

Method: 作者汇总了TrustNLP全部六届（2021-2026）144篇论文，基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust等成熟框架，将论文按其主题归入六个信任维度进行分类分析。同时追踪各维度随时间的变化趋势，并分析信任维度与模型能力涌现之间的共现关系。此外，与同期ACL、NAACL、EACL和EMNLP等主会约2000篇论文进行跨venue比较，以评估TrustNLP主题分布的领域代表性。

Result: 研究表明：第一批高影响力的对话模型发布激活了所有信任维度，而后续模型世代将关注重心转向真实性与安全对齐；真实性是增长最快的维度（2021-2022年几乎缺位，至2025-2026年占论文总量的37%）；公平性是最持久的主题；可解释性呈U型轨迹——先因事后可解释方法失去相关性而衰退，又在2026年通过机制可解释性重新崛起。跨venue比较显示TrustNLP的主题分布与领域平均水平高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该研究识别出四个结构性洞见，并为研究社区提出了切实可行的行动方向。整体表明可信NLP研究正随生成式AI能力的涌现而同步演进，从被动解释转向主动控制，其中真实性与安全对齐已成为该领域的核心关注点，同时机制可解释性作为新的可解释性范式正在复兴。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG，一个检索增强的合成对话生成框架，用于建模针对女性与女孩的暴力（VAWG）场景。该框架从人物画像、人口统计数据、官方犯罪定义和家庭凶杀审查案例构建事件时间线，生成多场景角色扮演对话，并通过激活引导的毒性控制确保内容安全。作者发布了包含200个场景、6000+多轮对话事件的数据集，并通过人工评估、LLM评审和下游任务验证了对话质量和领域保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话数据在敏感领域（如家庭暴力、胁迫、跟踪等）难以获取、发布和标注，受到隐私和法律约束的限制。现有工作主要聚焦于句子级在线毒性检测，缺乏对虐待作为关系性、时间展开现象的建模。这为合成对话生成提供了动机——在敏感领域中研究对话动态。

Method: ConVAWG框架包含四个核心步骤：(1) 从人物画像种子、英国国家统计局的人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索的国内凶杀审查（Domestic Homicide Review）案例构建场景；(2) 将场景转换为分层的社区事件时间线；(3) 生成多场景的角色扮演对话；(4) 对适当的语句应用激活引导的毒性控制（activation-steered toxicity control）。

Result: 发布了包含200个场景、超过6000个多轮对话事件的数据集，附带丰富的场景级、事件级和轮次级元数据。通过广泛的人类评估、LLM-as-Judge评估、消融实验和下游任务测试，验证了对话的高质量和高领域保真度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG展示了利用合成数据在一定程度上替代难以获取的真实敏感领域对话数据的可行性，填补了将虐待建模为多轮对话现象的研究空白，为VAWG相关研究提供了可用的高质量数据集和生成框架。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Safe Observation Capacity for Opponent Exploitation under Showdown Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09954)
*Jiaxing Guo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究扑克类博弈中因弃牌隐藏私有牌导致摊牌数据非随机缺失（MNAR）的问题。传统逐牌估计会收敛到选择分布，置信集随样本增长失去覆盖。作者提出"地板安全探针"（floor-safe probe）机制，通过序列形式流恢复被删失的弃牌质量，并以"安全观测容量"κ_ρ(I)刻画修复代价。在此基础上提出Safe Active De-censoring (SAD)算法，结合容量、公共异常路由与稳健部署，在多个博弈场景中验证了无条件安全性与条件性统计价值之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扑克类博弈中，弃牌动作隐藏了玩家的私有牌信息，导致摊牌数据具有非随机缺失（MNAR）特性。传统的逐牌估计器在这种情况下会收敛到有偏的选择分布，且其置信区间会随着样本量增大而失效（失去覆盖）。这本质上是一个"观测信息被选择性删失"的结构性问题，需要设计安全的观测机制来纠正偏差。

Method: 作者提出"地板安全探针"（floor-safe probe）机制：该机制将选定的线路驱动至摊牌阶段，揭示所有非弃牌的延续动作，并利用序列形式流（sequence-form flow）在"揭示认证历史"（reveal-certified histories）上恢复被删失的弃牌质量。通过"安全观测容量"κ_ρ(I)来量化在安全预算ρ下可实现的最大地板安全到达率，其前沿为凹的分段线性函数，原点斜率等于地板影子价格。在此基础上提出Safe Active De-censoring (SAD)算法，将容量与公共异常路由和稳健部署相结合。

Result: 当单手下揭示质量因子分解为安全到达率与对手延续概率时，匹配界限给出条件逐目标成本N=Õ(1/(κ_ρ(I)πε²))。在固定板面不分桶河牌子博弈中，构造的公共孪生（public twin）实现V=0.815的地板安全响应；公共通道仅认证蓝图地板，而种群揭示证据认证至少96%的V。在更广泛的合成对手种群中，公共与求解分组揭示纤维分别认证安全可利用差距的73%和91%中位数份额。

Conclusion: 独立地板审计覆盖所有评估的探针与响应，结果将无条件安全性（地板安全机制本身提供的保障）与条件性统计价值（主动揭示带来的统计推断价值）明确分离，表明前者成立而后者需要更多的条件约束和量化。

Abstract: In poker-like games, folds hide private cards, so showdown data are missing not at random. The usual per-card estimator converges to a selected distribution, and its confidence sets can lose coverage as the sample grows. A floor-safe probe changes the monitoring process: it drives a chosen line to showdown, reveals every non-fold continuation, and uses sequence-form flow to recover censored fold mass on reveal-certified histories. We price this repair through safe observation capacity $κ_ρ(I)$, the largest floor-safe reach rate at safety budget $ρ$. Its frontier is concave and piecewise linear, with origin slope equal to the floor's shadow price. When one-hand reveal mass factorizes into safe reach and opponent continuation, matching bounds give conditional per-target cost $N=\widetildeΘ(1/(κ_ρ(I)π\varepsilon^2))$ for a local censored-fiber direction. Safe Active De-censoring (SAD) combines capacity with public-anomaly routing and robust deployment; routing across targets remains heuristic. Evidence spans bucketed turn-river endgames, controlled instances, and a fixed-board unbucketed river subgame. In the latter, a constructed public twin admits a floor-safe response of value $V=0.815$; the audited public channel certifies only the blueprint floor, while population reveal evidence certifies at least $96\%$ of $V$. Across a broader synthetic opponent population, public and solved grouped reveal fibers certify median shares of $73\%$ and $91\%$ of the safe-exploitable gap. Independent floor audits cover every evaluated probe and response. The results separate unconditional safety from the conditional statistical value of active reveal.

</details>


### [177] [Efficiency Adjustments Break the Logarithmic Rank Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09984)
*Josue Ortega,Geng Zhao,Gabriel Ziegler*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究效率调整延迟接受（EADA）机制在i.i.d.匹配市场中的期望平均排名。作者证明EADA的期望平均排名至多为4log log n + O(1)，优于学生提议的延迟接受（DA）机制的对数阶期望平均排名。进一步地，作者将该结论推广到一大类机制：任何弱帕累托支配DA且帕累托有效的机制都能突破DA的对数屏障。这是对EADA及帕累托有效DA改进机制期望平均排名的首次渐近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生提议的延迟接受（DA）机制是匹配市场中最常用的机制之一，但已有研究表明其学生的期望平均排名为对数阶（O(log n)），这意味着排名靠后的学生可能获得较差的匹配。EADA等帕累托有效机制是否能在学生福利（以平均排名衡量）上取得渐近改进，此前缺乏理论保证。

Method: 作者在i.i.d.偏好匹配市场中对EADA的期望平均排名进行理论分析，推导出其渐近上界为4log log n + O(1)。同时，通过更弱的界O((log log n)^2)，将结论推广到所有弱帕累托支配DA且帕累托有效的机制。分析还扩展到配额有界的多对一市场和相关偏好下的随机市场。

Result: EADA的期望平均排名至多为4log log n + O(1)，在渐近阶上优于DA的对数阶。更一般地，任何弱帕累托支配DA且帕累托有效的机制的期望平均排名至多为O((log log n)^2)，突破了DA的对数屏障。该结论在具有有界配额的多对一市场和相关偏好随机市场中同样成立。

Conclusion: 该研究表明EADA以及其他帕累托有效的DA改进机制能够在学生福利（期望平均排名）上实现二阶对数级别的显著改进，为实践中采用EADA等改进机制提供了理论依据。这是首次从渐近意义上量化这类机制的福利优势。

Abstract: We study the expected average rank achieved by the Efficiency-Adjusted Deferred Acceptance (EADA) mechanism in i.i.d.\ matching markets. While student-proposing Deferred Acceptance gives students an expected average rank of logarithmic order, we prove that EADA's expected average rank is at most $4\log\log n+O(1)$. Therefore, EADA improves the asymptotic order of students' assignments.
  At the cost of a weaker bound, $O((\log\log n)^2)$, we extend this conclusion to a much larger class of mechanisms. Namely, every Pareto-efficient mechanism that weakly Pareto-dominates DA breaks DA's logarithmic barrier. These are the first asymptotic guarantees for the expected average rank of EADA and of the broader class of Pareto-efficient improvements of DA. The conclusions extend to many-to-one markets with bounded quotas and random markets with correlated preferences.

</details>


### [178] [Resolving Envy by Adding Goods with Bounded Supply: A Type-Count Dichotomy and Two-Agent Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10326)
*Chuang-Chieh Lin,Guillaume Fertin,Po-An Chen,Stéphane Vialette,Géraldine Jean,Emile Benoist,Colin Cleveland*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在额外物品池有界供给且无独立预算约束条件下，通过添加物品消除嫉妒（EEAG）问题的计算复杂性。针对二元加性估值，确立了尖锐的类型-数量二分法：仅有一种额外物品类型时可在多项式时间内求解（通过差分约束系统和Bellman-Ford算法）；恰好两种额外物品类型时是NP完全的，即使在正有限供给等受限条件下也成立。此外，还证明了即使只有两个代理、相同估值和单位供给的弱NP完全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEAG问题在实践中广泛存在（如资源分配、公平分配场景），但其计算复杂性尚未完全刻画。特别是Bentert等人遗留的两种物品类型情形未被解决，本文旨在填补这一空白并给出完整的类型-数量二分法。

Method: 对于单类型情形，将嫉妒约束形式化为差分约束系统，利用Bellman-Ford算法计算逐分量最小可行扩展，且该算法支持任意非负整数每份价值。对于两类型情形，通过从已知NP完全问题进行归约证明NP完全性；对双代理多类型情形，构造归约证明弱NP完全性。

Result: 确立了尖锐的二分法结果：单额外物品类型时多项式可解；恰好两种额外物品类型时NP完全（即使正有限供给且一种类型的批准者构成另一种的子集）。另外证明了双代理、相同加性估值、一个初始物品及多单位供给类型时的弱NP完全性。

Conclusion: EEAG的复杂性呈现非平凡的二分结构：单纯增加一个额外物品类型不会带来计算困难，但增加到两种类型即引起NP完全性爆炸；同时，即使代理数减少到两个，物品类型增多也会导致弱NP完全性，说明有界供给的困难性在"少量类型+多代理"与"少量代理+多类型"两个维度上都成立。

Abstract: We study envy elimination by adding goods (EEAG) when the additional pool has bounded supply and no separate budget bound. We establish a sharp type-count dichotomy for binary additive valuations. With one additional item type, EEAG is polynomial-time solvable for any number of agents. More generally, our algorithm permits arbitrary nonnegative integer per-copy values. The envy constraints form a system of difference constraints, and Bellman--Ford returns the componentwise least feasible extension. In contrast, with exactly two additional item types, EEAG is \textsf{NP}-complete even when both types have positive finite supply and the approvers of one type form a subset of the approvers of the other. This closes the two-type case left open by Bentert et al. Separately, we prove weak \textsf{NP}-completeness even for two agents with identical additive valuations, one initially endowed good, and a growing number of unit-supply item types. Thus, bounded-supply hardness appears both with two item-types and many agents and with two agents and many item-types.

</details>


### [179] [The Game of Marginal Utilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10373)
*Isaac M. Sonin,Georgy Gaitsgori,Yaakov Malinovsky*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类非合作资源分配博弈，其中m个玩家将固定资源分配给n个项目，收益函数结合了边际递减和竞争拥塞效应。作者证明了该博弈存在唯一纳什均衡，并给出了等边际原则刻画；发现玩家只在项目排序后的一段连续前缀中投资，且这些区间在不同玩家之间呈嵌套结构。在全活跃状态下，均衡可简化为求解单个标量非线性方程；同时提出了全局线性收敛的投影边际效用算法和利用嵌套边界结构的Block Pandora算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一类兼具收益递减与竞争拥塞效应的资源分配博弈中纳什均衡的存在性、唯一性、结构刻画及高效求解问题，为实际应用（如投资分配、资源竞争）提供理论保障和可计算算法。

Method: 采用博弈论分析，先证明均衡唯一性并通过等边际原则刻画均衡条件；通过对项目按a_i/b_i排序揭示投资区间的前缀性与嵌套性；在全活跃条件下将均衡约化为单个标量非线性方程；最后设计两种算法——投影边际效用算法（具有显式步长条件和全局线性收敛保障）以及Block Pandora算法（基于嵌套截断结构的重构与验证）。

Result: 证明了该博弈存在唯一纳什均衡；均衡中玩家按a_i/b_i排序后投资于连续前缀项目，玩家之间的投资区间呈嵌套结构，资源多的玩家在每个项目上的投资不弱于资源少的玩家。在全活跃状态下得到所有个体边际率、投资与收益的显式公式。提出的投影边际效用算法在显式步长条件下全局线性收敛，Block Pandora算法能高效重构并验证均衡。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和算法两方面完整刻画了这类拥塞型资源分配博弈的纳什均衡，既揭示了均衡的嵌套结构（连续活动区带），又提供了可操作的收敛算法，为该类博弈在实践中的求解与应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We study a noncooperative resource-allocation game in which $m$ players distribute fixed resources among $n$ projects and the payoff of player $j$ is given by \[ F^j(x) =
\sum_{i=1}^n \frac{a_i x_i^j}{b_i+\sum_{\ell=1}^m x_i^\ell}, \] where $a_i$ and $b_i$ are project parameters, while $x_i^j$ is the amount of resources player $j$ allocates to project $i$. This specification combines diminishing returns with congestion generated by competitors. We prove that the game has a unique Nash equilibrium and characterize it by an equimarginal principle. We show that, after the projects are ordered by $a_i/b_i$, each player invests in an initial segment of projects, and these segments are nested across players, so the equilibrium decomposes into consecutive activity zones; players with larger resources invest weakly more in every project. In the fully active regime, where every player invests in every project, we reduce the equilibrium to a single scalar nonlinear equation for the aggregate marginal-utility rate; all individual marginal rates, investments, and payoffs then follow from explicit formulas. We also provide a projected marginal-utility algorithm with global linear convergence under an explicit step-size condition, together with a structure-exploiting \textit{Block Pandora} algorithm that reconstructs and certifies the equilibrium conditional on a proposed nested cutoff structure.

</details>


### [180] [To EFX OR to MMS, That is the Question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10397)
*Hadi Hosseini,Payas Khurana,Shraddha Pathak,Rohit Vaish*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分割物品公平分配中"EFX∨MMS"这一逐智能体析取性公平概念（每位智能体至少满足EFX或MMS之一）。出乎意料的是，这种灵活性并未恢复存在性：作者构造了三智能体八件次模商品和三智能体七件次模苦役的反例，显著强化了近期EFX不可能性结果。正面结果包括：至多三种估值类型且其中一种为单例的加性混合物品存在性证明、超越加性的商品近似方案、以及加性苦役二估值类型下EFX∨MMS分配必然存在（而EFX和MMS各自均失败）的清晰分离。此外，相同加性估值下混合物品甚至可同时满足EFX∧MMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EFX分配的存在性问题已悬而未决近十年，作者探索是否可通过允许每位智能体灵活选择EFX或MMS中的一种作为公平性证书来恢复存在性，旨在扩大公平解的可行域并深入理解公平分配可行性的边界。

Method: （1）构造三智能体次模商品（8件）和次模苦役（7件）的反例证明EFX∨MMS不存在；（2）针对加性混合物品且至多三种估值类型（其中一种为单例）的情形给出存在性证明，并将其推广至商品加性情形之外；（3）为商品及三智能体苦役实例设计近似方案；（4）分析加性苦役二估值类型下EFX∨MMS与EFX、MMS各自之间的分离关系；（5）证明相同加性估值混合物品情形下EFX∧MMS同时满足。

Result: 主要结果为：次模设置下EFX∨MMS严格不可能（三智能体8件商品/7件苦役反例）；加性混合物品在特定估值类型约束下存在EFX∨MMS分配；苦役二估值类型下析取性成立而两个组成概念均失败，呈现完全分离；相同加性估值混合物品可满足合取性EFX∧MMS。

Conclusion: 允许智能体特定公平证书的灵活性（EFX∨MMS）虽然拓展了公平解的边界，但并未普遍恢复存在性，在次模设置下仍存在无法克服的不可能性；同时，某些特殊场景下析取性甚至可升级为合取性，表明公平性概念间的相互作用比预期更为复杂和微妙。

Abstract: We study the agent-wise disjunction of two central fairness notions for indivisible items, where every agent must be either envy-free up to any item (EFX) or maximin-share (MMS) satisfied. One might expect this flexibility to restore existence, especially because the existence of EFX itself resisted resolution for nearly a decade. Surprisingly, it does not. We construct counterexamples with three agents and eight submodular goods, and with three agents and seven submodular chores, significantly strengthening recent EFX impossibility results. On the positive side, we prove existence for additive mixed items with at most three valuation types when one type is a singleton. Our proof extends beyond additivity for goods and yields approximation schemes for goods and three-agent chores instances. We also identify a clean separation between the disjunction and its constituents: For additive chores with two valuation types, EFX and MMS are both known to fail, whereas an EFX$\vee$MMS allocation always exists. Finally, we show that identical additive valuations even admit the conjunction EFX$\wedge$MMS for mixed items. Overall, our results show that allowing flexibility in choosing agent-specific fairness certificates expands the frontier of fair solutions while also uncovering surprising impossibilities.

</details>


### [181] [Non-Existence of EFX Chore Allocations for Monotone Cost Functions with Binary Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10572)
*Zehan Lin,Shengxin Liu,Biaoshuai Tao,Shengwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究带有二元边际单调成本函数的不可分割杂务（chores）的EFX分配存在性问题。通过构造基于18个智能体、53个杂务的word gadget的两个反例，证明了在二元XOS成本和二元超模成本下，完整的EFX分配不一定存在，并用Lean 4形式化验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于不可分割物品（goods），二元边际单调函数的EFX分配存在性问题已有肯定答案；但对于杂务（chores），此问题仅在更受限的类别中已知答案，一般二元边际情形尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造了两个基于相同18智能体53杂务的word gadget的反例：一个针对二元XOS成本函数，另一个针对二元超模成本函数，均证明完整的EFX分配不必存在。最后使用Lean 4定理证明器对主要结果进行了形式化验证。

Result: 在两种成本函数类别（二元XOS和二元超模）下，均给出了不存在完整EFX分配的具体反例，表明此前关于goods的肯定结果不能直接推广到chores。所有主要结论均已在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Conclusion: 本文为杂务场景下二元边际成本的EFX分配存在性问题提供了否定的答案，同时展示了一种结合人工构造反例与形式化验证（Lean 4）的研究方法。

Abstract: We study the existence of envy-free up to any item (EFX) allocations of indivisible chores when agents have monotone cost functions with binary marginals. For indivisible goods, the corresponding existence question is known to have an affirmative answer for general monotone functions with binary marginals. For chores, however, the existence of EFX allocation was previously known only for more restricted classes, while the general binary-marginal case remained unresolved. In this paper, we provide two counterexamples based on the same 18-agent, 53-chore word gadget, with one cost profile for binary XOS costs and another for binary supermodular costs. In both cases, a complete EFX allocation need not exist. Finally, we formalize and verify our main results in Lean 4.

</details>


### [182] [A Consolidated Game Framework for Cooperative Defense Against Cross-Domain Cyber Attacks in Satellite-Enabled Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10873)
*Linan Huang,Peilong Liu,Xu Chen,Chunxiao Jiang,Linling Kuang,Jianhua Lu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三方安全博弈框架，用于解决卫星物联网多域架构中的跨域网络威胁。通过流定价机制激励IoT网络运营商（IoT-NO）阻止恶意数据包渗透至卫星域，并设计高效学习算法使IoT-NO和卫星服务提供商（SAT-SP）在激励不一致的情况下实现协同防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着卫星物联网的广泛应用，其跨域架构极易遭受跨域网络攻击。攻击者可利用被攻陷的IoT设备向数据流中注入恶意数据包，进而威胁卫星网络。单域防御无法有效应对跨域智能攻击，而量化跨域影响以及协调不同参与方之间的激励错位是系统性协同防御开发的主要挑战。

Method: 提出三方安全博弈框架，同时刻画地面域和卫星域中攻击与防御的影响。基于该博弈模型，设计流定价机制以最优激励IoT-NO阻止恶意数据包渗透入卫星域；随后提出高效学习算法，使IoT-NO能够确定理想流采样策略，同时使SAT-SP确定最优流定价策略。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的整合博弈框架在抵御跨域网络攻击方面具有有效性，并能促使激励不一致的IoT-NO与SAT-SP之间实现协同防御。

Conclusion: 该三方安全博弈框架成功解决了卫星物联网跨域防御中激励错位和跨域影响量化两大核心难题，为IoT设备、接入网络和卫星传输网络三个层面的细粒度协同防御提供了系统化方案。

Abstract: As the adoption of satellite-enabled Internet of Things (IoT) continues to rise, its intricate multidomain architecture becomes increasingly susceptible to cross-domain cyber threats. Attackers can exploit compromised IoT devices, inject malicious packets into data streams aggregated at the IoT gateway for satellite backhaul, and potentially endanger the satellite network during transmission by exploiting the hardware, software, and protocol vulnerabilities. Compared to single-domain defenses, cooperative defense at the IoT devices, IoT access network, and satellite transmission network provides fine-granularity defense against cross-domain intelligent attacks. However, quantifying cross-domain impacts and tilting incentive misalignment among different participants remain significant challenges, making systematic cooperative defense development a complex task. To address this, we develop a tripartite security game framework to characterize the impacts of attacks and defense methods across both the terrestrial and satellite domains. Leveraging this game model, we devise flow pricing to optimally motivate the IoT network operator (IoT-NO) to prevent malicious packet infiltration into the satellite domain. Subsequently, we propose efficient learning algorithms enabling both the IoT-NO to ascertain their ideal flow sampling strategies and the satellite service provider (SAT-SP) to determine optimal flow pricing. The simulation results corroborate the effectiveness of the consolidated game in counteracting cross-domain cyber attacks and facilitating cooperative defense between the IoT-NO and the SAT-SP with nonaligned incentives.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [A full software stack for epidemic disease management: Unlocking the joint potential of software technology and supercomputing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09933)
*Jonas Gilg,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Mariama Jaiteh,David Kerkmann,Niklas Medinger,Shahbaz Memon,Anna Wendler,Moritz Zeumer,Henrik Zunker,Maximilian Betz,Ralf Hannemann-Tamas,Jonas Immanuel Heinicke,Julian Litz,Achim Basermann,Cas Cremers,Manuel Dahmen,Andreas Gerndt,Jens Henrik Göbbert,Björn Hagemeier,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Berit Lange,Katharina Nöh,Sarah Straßburger,Michael Meyer-Hermann,Martin J. Kühn*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文指出尽管数学建模在COVID-19疫情期间发挥了重要作用，但许多关键流程仍依赖人工操作，制约了疫情响应速度。作者主张构建自动化、模块化的高性能计算基础设施，以支持快速部署传染病模型、动态集成数据源、确保安全合规，并强调遵循FAIR原则以提高软件的可重用性和可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情期间数学模型虽有效，但建模流程中专家咨询、模型执行、情景分析、报告生成和结果沟通等关键环节仍高度依赖人工，每次人工交互都会引入不可避免的延迟，限制了疫情快速演变时的响应能力。因此需要自动化流程和集成化软件模块来显著缩短响应时间。

Method: 提出构建稳健灵活的自动化HPC工作流计算基础设施，能够支持异构硬件和不断演进的传染病模型，动态集成多源数据，同时确保安全的用户与数据管理以符合数据保护法规，并以透明方式向决策者和公众呈现结果。

Result: 文章提出将最先进的科学软件与现代可扩展基础设施紧密结合，在必要时充分利用超级计算资源；同时强调未来疫情或大流行场景下的快速部署需遵循FAIR原则（可查找、可访问、可互操作、可重用），以保证软件的可重用性和可持续性。

Conclusion: 疫情防备应转向自动化工作流和无缝集成的软件模块，通过减少响应时间提升大流行缓解能力，而实现这一目标需要兼具高性能计算能力、安全合规性和FAIR原则遵守的现代软件基础设施。

Abstract: Infectious diseases remain a major threat to human societies. During the recent COVID-19 pandemic, mathematical modeling and extensive computer simulations proved highly effective in supporting public health experts and decision makers.
  Despite these advances, the full potential of modern modeling approaches and digital technologies has not yet been realized. Many critical tasks -- including expert consultations, model execution, scenario analyses, report preparation, and result communication -- still relied heavily on manual, human-driven processes with each manual interaction introducing avoidable delays and limiting responsiveness during rapidly evolving outbreaks.
  Pandemic preparedness should opt for automated workflows and seamlessly integrated software modules that can improve pandemic mitigation capabilities by substantially reducing response times. For this step, we require robust and flexible computational infrastructure capable of supporting heterogeneous hardware and continuously evolving infectious-disease models. In addition, data sources need to be dynamically integrated. Managing such demands needs infrastructure that supports automated high-performance computing (HPC) workflows. Beyond computational performance, software infrastructure must ensure secure user and data management to comply with data-protection regulations and provide clear, transparent presentation of results to both decision makers and the public.
  Meeting the aforementioned challenges requires tight integration of state-of-the-art scientific software with modern, scalable infrastructure that can leverage supercomputing resources when necessary. For rapid deployment in future epidemic or pandemic scenarios, adherence to the FAIR principles for research software is critical to ensure reusability and sustainability.

</details>


### [184] [HyperShape: Hyperelasticity Across Diverse Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09938)
*Leo Widmer,Sidaty El Hadramy,Stéphane Cotin,Philippe Claude Cattin*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperShape，一个可扩展的框架，用于生成合成形状及其对应的超弹性模拟数据，系统地评估神经算子在不同域（分布内、分布外、合成到真实迁移）上的泛化能力。实验发现神经算子在简单形状上表现良好，但随着形状复杂度、几何多样性和边界条件变化增加，性能显著下降且需要大量训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超弹性变形对域几何和边界条件高度敏感，神经算子需要跨两者进行泛化。然而现有神经算子基准只依赖简单或少数几何形状，无法严格评估此能力，因此需要更全面的基准框架。

Method: 构建HyperShape框架，生成2D和3D合成形状及其超弹性仿真数据，具备可调的复杂度和可控的形状变化。在该框架下，对多种最先进神经算子在不同形状分布上进行系统评估，涵盖分布内、分布外、合成到真实迁移三种场景。

Result: 神经算子在简单形状上表现良好，但随着形状复杂度、几何多样性和边界条件变异性增加，性能持续且可预测地退化，需要大量训练数据。该基准揭示了当前神经算子在此领域的泛化局限。

Conclusion: HyperShape作为开放可扩展的基准，能够随领域发展不断添加新几何、材料模型、加载条件和评估设置，用于验证超弹性代理模型；当前结果也凸显了进一步模型开发的必要性。

Abstract: Hyperelastic deformations are highly sensitive to domain geometry and boundary conditions, making generalization across both a critical capability for neural operators applied to these problems. However, existing benchmarks for neural operators on hyperelasticity rely on simple or few geometries, which makes it difficult to assess this capability rigorously. To address this gap, we introduce HyperShape, an extensible framework designed to generate synthetic shapes and their corresponding hyperelastic simulation data, producing a suite of 2D and 3D datasets with adjustable complexity and controllable shape variations. This design enables systematic assessment of generalization across in-distribution, out-of-distribution, and synthetic-to-real transfer settings. Using this framework, we evaluated the performance of several state-of-the-art neural operators over diverse shape distributions. Our findings reveal that neural operators perform well on simple shapes but struggle as shape complexity, geometric diversity, and boundary condition variability increase, requiring large amounts of training data in such regimes. Performance degrades consistently and predictably with geometric complexity highlighting the need for further model development. As an open and extensible benchmark, HyperShape is designed to grow alongside the field: new geometries, material models, loading conditions, and evaluation settings can be easily incorporated to validate hyperelastic surrogate models.

</details>


### [185] [The Researcher's Guide to HPC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09953)
*C. Nicole Avans,Evan Drake Suggs,Riley Shipley,Dennis Jump,Anthony Skjellum*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文档旨在降低学习超级计算机和数据中心网络技术的门槛，涵盖了从网络编程API、控制平面、物理互连到链路层设备的全部层次，并通过劳伦斯利弗莫尔国家实验室的El Capitan超级计算机架构进行实例讲解，为初学者提供丰富的参考文献和起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络是几乎所有计算系统中的关键基础设施，在HPC系统中尤为重要。然而，构成超级计算机和数据中心网络的许多硬件和软件技术很少在课堂上教授，也未被教科书广泛覆盖，因此学习这些技术的门槛较高。

Method: 本文档通过系统性地覆盖通信和网络编程API、网络控制平面技术、物理互连和链路层设备等所有层次的内容，并以El Capitan超级计算机的实际架构为例进行具体说明，同时提供大量参考文献以支持深入探索。

Result: 成功提供了一个全面且易于理解的学习起点，通过将各层次网络技术与El Capitan超级计算机的架构相结合，使抽象概念得以具象化，并提供了丰富的参考资源供进一步研究。

Conclusion: 该文档作为一种教学资源，有效降低了HPC网络技术的学习门槛，对于希望在超级计算和高端网络领域深入学习的研究者和学生具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Networks are critical infrastructure in nearly every computing system, but they are particularly important in High Performance Computing (HPC) systems, where users tackle problems at massive scale. Unfortunately, many of the hardware and software technologies that comprise supercomputer and datacenter networks are not widely taught in classes, nor are they extensively covered by textbooks or other educational documents outside of their primary sources. The purpose of this document is to provide a starting point to learn about these technologies by lowering the barrier to understanding and providing an abundance of references for further research. Covered topics include all levels of communication and network programming APIs, network control plane technologies, physical interconnects, and link-layer devices which are then brought to life in discussion of the architecture of El Capitan, a leadership class supercomputer created by Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory.

</details>


### [186] [pyRMV: Reusable, Cross-model Validation for Computational Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09956)
*Hugo Dictus,Eduard Subert,Armando Romani,Henry Markram*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种与模型无关的验证方法及Python库，用于验证小鼠初级视觉皮层计算模型，通过将模型视为系统属性的预测器而非靶系统的替代品，使验证易于推广到不同模型，推动了计算科学中模型验证标准化的关键一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算科学中模型验证缺乏标准化是当前最重要的问题。现有验证方法往往与特定模型紧密耦合，难以推广，因此需要一种模型无关、易于编写且可广泛适用的验证框架。

Method: 提出模型无关的验证方法：将模型视为系统属性的生成器而非靶系统的替代品，据此编写验证套件；同时提供一个包含小鼠初级视觉皮层模型系列验证的Python库。

Result: 该模型无关方法使验证能轻松泛化到不同模型，且编写相对简单。论文还概述了该方法的若干局限性及可能的应对方案。

Conclusion: 这一工作在推动计算科学中模型验证标准化方面迈出了重要一步，为计算模型验证提供了一种通用、可扩展的框架。

Abstract: We present a model-agnostic approach to the validation of computational models, and a python library containing a suite of validations for mouse primary visual cortex models. By viewing the model not as a stand-in for the target system, but instead as a generator of predicted system properties, this approach allows validations to easily generalize to many different models, while also making them comparatively simple to write. Several limitations of this approach are outlined alongside possible means of addressing them. This presents an important step in addressing what we consider the most important problem in computational science: the standardization of model validation.

</details>


### [187] [Optimizing Parameterized Physics-Informed Neural Networks to Solve Multilayered Static Linear Elastic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09981)
*Joseph Lim,Hanbo Song,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化物理信息神经网络（P2INNs）框架，替代传统有限元方法（FEM），用于多层材料受控变形的高效设计与优化，在满足精度要求的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业级有限元方法（FEM）在多层材料设计中计算成本极高、耗时严重，极大限制了设计空间的探索和基于梯度的优化，亟需一种更高效的替代方案。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：I. 基于Hex8单元和三线性基函数的FEM基线；II. 跨可变材料刚度与层厚参数化的P2INN位移场模型；III. 以FEM为参照的评估。PINN通过Navier-Cauchy残差强制执行静态线性弹性，并采用分层PDE分解、界面连续性惩罚和顺应性感知缩放等训练策略来优先保证物理一致性。

Result: 单层纯物理驱动配置下，PINN的平均体积MAE为1.56%，最差情况为2.87%；三层受控监督训练配置下，平均体积MAE为2.53%，最差情况为4.66%，满足约5%最差体积MAE的初步设计空间探索目标。

Conclusion: 训练后的P2INN是轻量模型，具有简单前向传播，可作为传统FEM的替代方案。通过大幅减少昂贵的FEM评估需求，该方法可加速先进多层结构的逆向设计与优化，适用于高载荷或潜在碰撞场景下的防护结构领域。

Abstract: Designing multilayered materials for controlled deformation is heavily bottlenecked by the immense computational costs and time of traditional, industrial-grade finite element methods (FEM). This excessive expense severely limits design space exploration and gradient-based optimization. To streamline the workflow, we propose a framework for parameterized physics-informed neural networks (P2INNs). The framework encompasses three core components: I. a FEM baseline with a Hex8 element and trilinear basis functions, II. a P2INN displacement field model across variable material stiffness and layer thicknesses, and III. FEM-referenced evaluation. The PINN enforces static linear elasticity via Navier-Cauchy residuals with training strategies that prioritize physics, including layerwise PDE decomposition, interface continuity penalty, and compliance aware scaling. For one-layer, pure physics-driven configurations, the PINN achieves a mean volume MAE of 1.56% and worst-case volume MAE of 2.87% against the FEM benchmark. For three-layer configurations with controlled supervised training, the PINN achieves a mean volume MAE of 2.53% and worst-case volume MAE of 4.66%, meeting the near-5% worst-case volume-MAE target for preliminary design-space exploration. The trained P2INN model remains a lightweight model with a simple forward pass for future calculations, creating an alternative to traditional FEM. By drastically reducing the need for expensive FEM evaluations, this approach could accelerate the inverse design and optimization of advanced layered architectures for protective structures in various load-heavy or potentially collision-heavy fields.

</details>


### [188] [OpenPM: Auditable Point-in-Time Evaluation for LLM Portfolio-Management Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09988)
*Xinying Cai,Minghao Guo,Jiahe Liu,Jiaojiao Han,Bangwei Guo,Yitao Long,Yuxuan Chen,Bohan Wu,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Raymond Li*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenPM，一个可审计、时点一致的LLM投资组合管理代理评估框架，通过防泄漏数据、强约束风险指令和审计产物来消除LLM交易代理评估中的虚高结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM交易代理的已报告结果常因前瞻泄漏、乐观成交假设和未被强制执行的"口头"风险约束而虚高，缺乏可靠的评估框架。

Method: OpenPM框架中，代理在S&P 500股票池上管理100万美元多头仓位，使用五分钟间隔的市场数据，所有记录严格为时点可得；自然语言风险指令被转换为类型化约束并在执行组合上强制执行；每次运行生成审计产物（污染证明、成本敏感性曲线、约束遵守报告）。构建了层级分配器参考代理：类型化分析师评分候选股、构造LLM提出权重、确定性评判器保证可行性；通过一次性捕获分析师证据并在不同构造模型间重放以隔离构造器行为。

Result: 在短期窗口案例研究中，更强的构造器相对于等权重仅有温和且依赖模型的收益；分析师质量比构造器选择更重要，换手率是主要成本驱动因素。

Conclusion: 所有回报仅是在单一冻结窗口上无市场冲击约束的上界，而非经过验证的alpha，强调结果需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to read markets, assess risk, and allocate capital. However, reported results for LLM trading agents can be inflated by look-ahead leakage, optimistic execution, and risk mandates that are described but not enforced. We present OpenPM, an auditable point-in-time evaluation framework for LLM portfolio-management agents. In OpenPM, an agent manages a \$1M long-only book over the S\&P 500 universe using market data at five-minute intervals. Every record visible to the agent must be available at the decision time. Natural-language risk mandates are converted into typed constraints and enforced on the executed portfolio. Each run produces audit artifacts, including a contamination certificate, a cost-sensitivity curve, and a constraint-adherence report. We also build a reference agent named the tiered allocator, where typed analysts score candidates, a constructor LLM proposes weights, and a deterministic critic guarantees feasibility. We isolate constructor behavior by capturing analyst evidence once and replaying it across constructor models. In our short-window case study, stronger constructors show modest and model-dependent gains over equal weighting on the same pool, but analyst quality matters more than constructor choice, and turnover is the main cost driver. All returns are upper bounds on a single frozen window without market impact, not validated alpha.

</details>


### [189] [VeriFin: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Verifying LLM-Generated Financial Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10213)
*Bethel Hall,Sachi Shome,William Eiers*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: VeriFin是一个神经符号验证框架，用于金融数值问答，通过将操作数锚定在XBRL事实中、从问题/连接库/指标定义推导计算公式，并使用Z3求解器验证LLM生成的数值声明。在600题的XBRLFiling基准和FinanceBench上，VeriFin实现了零错误接受率，而基线模型接受了6到92个错误声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融文件问答中经常生成看似合理但实际错误的数值声明，其错误可能源于错误的报告期间、单位、行项目或计算公式。仅仅重新检查算术不足以验证这类声明，因为验证需要锚定相关事实、确定授权的计算方法，以及判断候选值是否从两者中正确推导出来。现有方法缺乏对源数据的可靠锚定和可审计的验证机制。

Method: VeriFin采用神经符号混合框架：(1) 将操作数锚定在已申报的XBRL事实中；(2) 从问题文本、文件链接库或文档化指标定义中推导计算方法；(3) 使用Z3 SMT求解器验证声明是否成立。当声明不一致时，利用求解器推导的不可满足核（unsatisfiable cores）识别冲突的事实、公式和候选值，从而实现有针对性的修复。

Result: 在XBRLFiling基准（600题，来自28家美国公司10-K文件）上，VeriFin不接受任何错误声明，而基线方法接受了6到92个错误声明。在FinanceBench的67个声明上，基线接受了4到21个错误声明而VeriFin为零。零错误接受结果在不同答案生成模型下保持一致。求解器推导的反馈在真实召回中实现了最高69.9%的修复恢复率。

Conclusion: 源锚定的符号验证能够为LLM生成的金融声明提供可靠且可审计的接受控制。VeriFin证明了将神经网络的语义理解与符号推理的形式保证相结合，可以显著提升金融问答系统中数值声明验证的可信度。

Abstract: Large language models often produce plausible numerical claims from financial filings while using the wrong reporting period, unit, line item, or formula. Verifying such claims requires more than rechecking arithmetic: a verifier must ground the relevant facts, establish the authorized calculation, and determine whether the candidate value follows from both. We propose VeriFin, a neurosymbolic verification framework for numerical financial question answering that grounds operands in filed XBRL facts, derives calculations from the question, filing linkbases, or documented metric definitions, and checks claims using Z3. When a claim is inconsistent, solver-derived unsatisfiable cores identify the conflicting facts, formula, and candidate value, enabling targeted repair. We evaluate VeriFin on XBRLFiling, a new 600-question benchmark constructed from 10-K filings of 28 U.S. companies, and on FinanceBench. On fixed candidate pools shared by all verification methods, VeriFin accepts none of the incorrect claims, whereas the baselines accept 6 to 92 incorrect claims on XBRLFiling's 600 claims and 4 to 21 incorrect claims on FinanceBench's 67 claims. The zero false-accept result persists across multiple answer-generator models. Solver-derived feedback further improves repair, achieving up to 69.9% recovery among true catches. These results show that source-grounded symbolic verification can provide a reliable and auditable acceptance control for LLM-generated financial claims.

</details>


### [190] [The Kuramoto Neural Operator: Learning to Solve PDEs via Coupled Oscillator Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10234)
*Petr Badolia,Leonid Obukhov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出Kuramoto Neural Operator (KNO)，一种基于耦合振子系统连续极限的算子学习架构，用于偏微分方程求解。KNO通过潜在振荡器场的演化来表征解算子，在多个PDE基准上取得优于现有方法的性能，并通过消融实验和同步性分析揭示了模型工作机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习架构大多在固定基函数中表示解算子，这虽适合全局结构，但对局部物理空间相互作用主导的现象（如局部相互作用驱动的PDE）不够适用。作者观察到耦合振荡器系统的连续极限可描述广泛PDE类别，因此探索基于振子动力学的新视角来构建算子学习模型。

Method: KNO通过潜在场中相互作用的振荡器演化来表示PDE解。其核心思想是将解算子的学习转化为对耦合格子振子系统的连续极限动力学建模，利用振子间的耦合来捕捉物理空间中的局部相互作用。模型包含多个架构组件，并通过消融实验逐一量化各组件贡献。

Result: 在多样化PDE基准上，KNO相较于现有方法取得更强的预测性能。消融研究量化了每个架构组件的贡献。此外，预测误差与潜在振荡器的集体动力学密切相关，误差随振荡器同步程度呈系统性变化。

Conclusion: 基于耦合振子动力学的算子学习方法有效且具有解释性：模型性能与潜在振荡器的同步行为存在可量化的关联，这为理解算子学习模型的内部机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: Operator learning is a rapidly advancing area of computational science. It is particularly well suited to problems where a partial differential equation (PDE) must be solved repeatedly under varying physical configurations. Most existing architectures represent the solution operator in a fixed basis. While this assumption is well aligned with global structures, it is less suitable for phenomena governed by local interactions in physical space. We explore an alternative perspective motivated by the observation that the continuum limit of coupled oscillator systems can describe a broad class of PDEs. Building on this idea, we introduce the Kuramoto Neural Operator (KNO), which represents the solution through the evolution of a latent field of interacting oscillators. Across a diverse collection of PDE benchmarks, KNO achieves strong predictive performance, with improvements over competing approaches. Our experimental evaluation also includes an extensive ablation study that quantifies the contribution of each architectural component incorporated into KNO. Furthermore, we show that the model's prediction error is closely linked to the collective dynamics of the latent oscillators. It varies systematically with their degree of synchronization, providing insights into the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Forecasting: Recasting Volatility Control as a Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10375)
*Hongji Pu,Leyang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出VolRouter，一个将波动率控制建模为状态条件化路由问题的模块化框架。它通过状态推理、开关审查和配对选择三个阶段，在不同市场条件下动态选择最优的估计器-控制器组合，在多个资产设置中显著提升了Sharpe比率并降低了回撤和CVaR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的波动率控制方法通常依赖固定的波动率估计器或预定义的控制规则，无法适应不断变化的市场条件。随着风险管理需求在不同市场状态下变化，需要一种能够动态选择最优控制策略的框架。

Method: VolRouter框架包含三个核心组件和三个阶段。首先将市场条件总结为控制相关的状态画像，然后通过状态推理（state inference）、开关审查（switch review）和配对选择（pair selection）三个阶段进行路由。路由决策模块可采用基于规则、可学习或基于LLM的实现，但资产组合的操作仍由预定义的控制策略生成，保持模块化灵活性。

Result: 在S&P 500、Multi-Asset、Bitcoin和USDT四种波动率控制设置中评估，VolRouter在四种设置中的三种达到最高Sharpe比率。S&P 500上Sharpe从0.952提升至1.222，最大回撤从15.10%降至12.58%，每日CVaR从1.76%降至1.32%；Multi-Asset上Sharpe从1.498提升至1.540，CVaR从1.56%降至1.18%。消融实验表明改进来自相对策略评估和选择性持续切换，而非简单扩大策略库。

Conclusion: 波动率控制可以被重新表述为一个策略选择问题，当风险管理要求随市场状态变化而变化时，动态路由估计器-控制器组合比固定策略更有效。USDT作为边界案例验证了在低波动资产中更简单的状态感知选择器仍具有竞争力。

Abstract: Volatility control converts risk estimates into portfolio exposure, yet existing approaches often rely on a fixed volatility estimator or a pre-defined control rule that may not adapt to changing market conditions. We propose VolRouter, a modular framework that formulates volatility control as state-conditioned routing over estimator-controller pairs. VolRouter first summarizes market conditions into a control-relevant state profile and then performs routing through three stages: state inference, switch review, and pair selection. The Router can be implemented using rule-based, learnable, or LLM-based decision modules, while portfolio actions remain generated by predefined control policies. We evaluate VolRouter across S&P 500, Multi-Asset, Bitcoin, and USDT volatility-control settings. VolRouter achieves the highest Sharpe ratio in three of four settings. On S&P 500, it improves Sharpe from 0.952 for RV + Naive Scaling to 1.222 while reducing maximum drawdown from 15.10% to 12.58% and daily CVaR from 1.76% to 1.32%. On Multi-Asset, it improves Sharpe from 1.498 to 1.540 and reduces CVaR from 1.56% to 1.18%. Bitcoin shows similar improvements in risk-adjusted performance, while USDT provides a boundary case where simpler state-aware selectors remain competitive. Ablation and sensitivity analyses show that the improvement comes from relative policy evaluation and selective persistent switching rather than simply expanding the policy library. These results suggest that volatility control can be viewed as a policy-selection problem when risk management requirements vary across market states.

</details>


### [192] [FlowGRN+: Improving Gene Regulatory Network Inference by Spline Fitting and Manifold Projection in Conditional Flow Matching (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10407)
*Tsz Pan Tong,Jun Pang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: FlowGRN+ 是 FlowGRN 的改进版本，通过将样条拟合集成到条件流匹配（CFM）框架中以生成更稳定的参考轨迹，并引入投影方案解决样条拟合中的过冲问题，从而提升细胞轨迹重建的时间连贯性和基因调控网络（GRN）推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 scRNA-seq 数据推断基因调控网络（GRN）仍然面临高维性和dropout等挑战。尽管 FlowGRN 通过条件流匹配取得了有前景的结果，但其重建轨迹的时间连贯性不足，且依赖人工检查，阻碍了下游应用和可重复性。

Method: FlowGRN+ 在 CFM 框架中引入样条拟合（spline fitting）以生成更稳定的参考轨迹用于训练，从而提高学习动力学的时间连贯性；同时提出一种投影方案，将样条切线投影到数据流形的局部切空间，以解决样条拟合中的过冲（overshooting）问题。

Result: 在 BEELINE 基准上进行评估，FlowGRN+ 在提高轨迹平滑度的同时保持了有竞争力的 GRN 推断性能。

Conclusion: FlowGRN+ 为从 scRNA-seq 数据重建细胞轨迹和推断 GRN 提供了一个实用框架，其方法见解也可能对其他基于 CFM 的细胞动力学模型具有参考价值。

Abstract: Gene regulatory networks (GRNs) are fundamental in understanding cellular dynamics and underlying mechanisms during development and disease. Although scRNA-seq technologies have enabled the collection of vast numbers of gene expression profiles at single-cell resolution, inferring GRNs from scRNA-seq data remains a significant challenge due to high dimensionality and dropout. Recently, FlowGRN has shown promising results in reconstructing cell trajectories and inferring GRNs by applying conditional flow matching (CFM) to learn the cell dynamics. However, FlowGRN still faces limitations in the temporal coherence of reconstructed dynamics and relies on human inspection, which hinders downstream applications and reproducibility. In this paper, we propose FlowGRN+, an improved version of FlowGRN that integrates spline fitting into the CFM framework to generate more stable reference trajectories for training, thereby improving the temporal coherence of the learned dynamics. To address overshooting in spline fitting, we further introduce a projection scheme that projects spline tangents onto the local tangent space of the data manifold. We evaluate FlowGRN+ on the BEELINE benchmark and show improved trajectory smoothness with a competitive GRN inference performance. FlowGRN+ provides a practical framework for reconstructing cell trajectories and inferring GRNs from scRNA-seq data, and the insights from this work may also be useful for other CFM-based models of cellular dynamics.

</details>


### [193] [Geometry-aware neural causal discovery for large-scale spatiotemporal systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10466)
*Haoyang Yan,Kaiqi Zhao,Weiping Wang,Yunpeng Wang,Xiaolei Ma*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: GeoDCD是一个几何感知的神经因果发现框架，利用空间坐标初始化可学习层次结构，并通过输入输出雅可比敏感性分析将训练好的非线性预测器转化为时变有向图。在混沌Lorenz-96动力学上F1达0.99，结构汉明距离较最强神经基线降低36.8%，在10512节点海平面气压网格上仍保持近似线性可扩展性，同时在真实观测中识别出环流一致的门户、厄尔尼诺/拉尼娜相关的重组织以及电动汽车与道路交通系统的耦合影响方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度时空因果发现面临三重挑战：变量在物理空间中嵌入、候选交互随系统规模二次增长、因果结构随系统状态动态变化。现有方法难以同时应对这些挑战，需要一种能够利用空间几何信息、可扩展且能捕捉时变因果结构的框架。

Method: GeoDCD采用三项关键技术：(1) 利用空间坐标初始化一个可学习的层次结构，将几何信息编码到模型架构中；(2) 通过训练非线性预测器，将其输入输出雅可比矩阵的敏感性分析转化为随时间变化的因果有向图；(3) 边权定义为神经Granger敏感性而非干预效应，适合缺乏干预实验的场景。

Result: 在Lorenz-96混沌动力学上，GeoDCD达到0.99的F1分数，结构汉明距离比最强神经基线减少36.8%；即使空间坐标无信息时仍优于扁平基线。运行时间近似线性增长，成功应用于10512节点的海平面气压网格；在真实观测中识别出环流一致的门户，解析了厄尔尼诺/拉尼娜事件导致的因果重组织，并分离了电动汽车与道路系统之间的双向影响方向。

Conclusion: GeoDCD证明了将空间几何先验纳入神经因果发现框架可以同时提升准确性和可扩展性，适用于大规模物理系统和真实观测数据。其神经Granger敏感性边定义为在缺乏干预条件下进行机制性假设生成提供了实用途径，但需注意其识别的是预测敏感性而非严格因果效应。

Abstract: Causal discovery at large spatiotemporal scale is difficult: variables are physically embedded, candidate interactions grow quadratically with system size, and causal structure changes with system state. We introduce GeoDCD, a geometry-aware neural framework that uses spatial coordinates to initialize a learnable hierarchy and converts a trained nonlinear predictor into time-varying directed graphs through input-output Jacobian sensitivity analysis. On chaotic Lorenz-96 dynamics, GeoDCD attains an F1 score of 0.99, reducing structural Hamming distance by 36.8% relative to the strongest neural baseline, and still leads flat baselines when coordinates are uninformative. Runtime scales approximately linearly over the evaluated range, enabling discovery on a 10,512-node sea-level-pressure grid. Applied to observations, GeoDCD identifies circulation-consistent gateways, resolves El Nino/La Nina-dependent reorganization, and separates energy-to-traffic from traffic-to-energy influence in coupled electric-vehicle and road systems. Edges are neural-Granger sensitivities rather than interventional effects, positioning GeoDCD for mechanistic hypothesis generation where interventions are unavailable.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂生物系统中的应用，从数据分析演变为自主AI引导实验。该系统基于49通道植物传感器网络（涵盖多光谱、电化学和介电模态），处理生物物理数据以评估植物生理状态，并通过闭环架构触发硬件执行器来优化微气候、执行表型分析或诱导受控胁迫。在垂直农场和单株装置上通过三个案例验证，生产级部署中智能体实现多参数优化：最小时间模式将生产周期缩短35%，能量优化模式节能18%，并自主发现暗诱导叶绿素积累策略实现67.9%的节能。该框架将LLM转化为数字农业的自主副驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统植物生理研究依赖人工参与的分析和决策流程，存在专家劳动密集、计算成本高、时间效率低等瓶颈。作者旨在构建一个由LLM驱动的闭环AI-生物接口，从人类在环分析转向自主控制，实现数据驱动的复杂生物系统与生态系统的探索，提升数字农业的成本效益比。

Method: 构建由49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电模态）驱动的LLM框架。系统处理生物物理遥测数据，评估植物生理状态，通过自然语言实时向专家和非专家提供解读，并将决策转化为硬件执行器指令以优化微气候、执行表型协议或诱导受控胁迫。采用闭环架构，实现从数据采集、LLM分析与决策到物理执行的自主循环。通过三个案例（垂直农场、单株装置、生产级部署）进行验证，智能体以2小时间隔调节全光谱、450nm和660nm光照，在最小时间与能量优化两种模式下运行。

Result: 在生产级部署中，LLM智能体成功执行多参数优化，在生物量积累、叶绿素含量和能耗之间取得平衡。相比周期性对照：最小时间模式将生产周期缩短35%；能量优化模式通过利用生理惰性（光脉冲）节能18%，仅以微幅增加培养时间为代价。此外，智能体自主开发出前所未有的暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%的节能效果。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM从数据分析工具转变为数字农业中的自主副驾驶（autonomous co-pilot），直接建立AI-生物接口。它能降低专家劳动和计算约束，改善成本-价值比，并展现出自主发现科学策略的能力，为复杂生物系统和生态系统的数据驱动探索开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [195] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOT（Sampling Policy Observation Tree），一种模型无关、基于采样的DRL策略解释框架。SPOT通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态，构建可解释的有限时域决策树，提供策略动作偏好及其下游演化的经验表征，并给出渐近恢复最可能动作的理论保证，在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域验证了其超越单时间步特征归因方法的解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习（DRL）智能体在复杂环境中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释。现有的单时间步特征归因方法无法揭示策略的下游行为演化，需要一种能够同时反映动作偏好和未来轨迹的模型无关解释框架。

Method: SPOT框架的核心思想是：利用策略和环境模拟器，通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态，构建一个有限时域的采样策略观测树。该树以经验方式表征策略的动作偏好及其可能的下游演化路径。作者同时提供了理论分析，证明SPOT能够渐近恢复策略的唯一最可能动作，并刻画了高熵策略下的不一致行为。

Result: 在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域的案例研究中，SPOT的树状表征成功用于检查策略偏好、比较替代未来轨迹，并揭示了单时间步特征归因方法无法观测到的下游行为。

Conclusion: SPOT为DRL策略提供了一种可解释、有理论保证的采样树框架，弥补了传统特征归因方法在揭示策略下游行为方面的不足，适用于广泛的模型无关策略解释场景。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [196] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIDAS框架，通过互信息解缠和不确定性感知融合来应对多模态情感分析中的不完整模态数据问题，在三个基准数据集上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法假设输入模态完整，但实际应用中常遇到模态缺失或不完整的情况。现有方法依赖数据填充和启发式协调约束，无法有效从不完整的多模态数据中提取和利用任务相关信息。

Method: MIDAS采用变分建模策略，将每个模态表示为多元高斯潜变量，并分解为共享因子和独占因子。设计minimax目标：最小化共享空间与独占空间之间的互信息以实现稳定解缠，同时最大化跨模态共享空间之间的互信息以增强语义对齐。此外引入不确定性感知融合机制，利用后验方差作为可靠性指标自适应加权融合潜特征。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，MIDAS在各种不完整设置下相比竞争基线取得了强劲且一致的性能提升，证明了其在不完整数据场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过互信息解缠与不确定性感知融合的统一框架，有效解决了不完整多模态数据下的情感分析问题，在多样化的不完整场景中展现出显著的性能和鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [197] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了绿色AI、绿色深度学习和优化技术以减少AI模型的碳排放，并通过CPU实验评估六个DL模型在多层标签分类任务中的碳排放，发现训练阶段是排放主要来源，且模型复杂度与精度提升不成比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML模型特别是大规模深度学习模型在提供强大能力的同时带来高能耗和碳排放，环境问题日益突出，需要系统性地审视和量化其环境影响，并为可持续AI设计提供指导。

Method: 采用系统综述方法综述绿色AI/绿色DL及优化技术，比较多种碳测量工具；同时进行实证研究，在CPU环境中实现六个深度学习模型执行多层标签分类任务，量化和比较总碳排放及各生命周期阶段的排放贡献。

Result: 训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源；模型架构复杂度增加并不意味着精度成比例提升，揭示了预测性能与环境成本之间的非对称关系。

Conclusion: 需要在模型选择和AI系统设计中整合可持续性考量，在预测性能和环境影响之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [198] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCBM，一个不确定性门控的关系推理框架，用于概念瓶颈模型（CBM）。ReCBM在瓶颈层中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），并利用不确定性引导这些关系的细化，从而在概念状态不可靠（如缺失或翻转）时仍能进行稳健推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBM变体虽然改进了概念表示、不确定性估计和依赖建模，但在概念状态不可靠的情况下进行稳健推理的问题仍未充分探索。误导性的语义证据会通过瓶颈层传播，损害模型解释能力和下游预测性能。

Method: ReCBM包含三个核心设计：(1) 在瓶颈层中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），明确概念间的证据交换方式；(2) 利用不确定性作为门控信号，调节每个概念在关系推理过程中的贡献权重；(3) 通过不确定性引导关系细化，实现鲁棒的概念推理。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，ReCBM在概念缺失和翻转的情况下改善了概念恢复和任务恢复性能；支持不确定性感知的测试时干预；能提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集而不降低下游性能。

Conclusion: ReCBM证明了将语义概念关系与不确定性门控相结合，能够显著增强CBM在不可靠概念状态下的鲁棒性，同时保持可解释性和下游任务性能。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>


### [199] [Automating and Scaling Behavioral Scientific Research on AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10030)
*Soo Yong Lee,Jongha Lee,Jaewan Chun,Hyunjin Hwang,Fanchen Bu,Ziv Ben-Zion,Taekwan Kim,Denny Borsboom,Jaemin Yoo,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AEROBAT，这是首个能够自动完成AI智能体行为科学研究的通用多智能体系统，可自动执行从假设生成、实验设计、实验执行到行为评估和报告撰写的完整研究流程，并通过12个目标行为、79个假设、1240个受控实验和23512轮模拟验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在复杂环境中被广泛部署，理解其行为变得至关重要。然而，现有的AI智能体行为科学研究仍高度依赖人工，过程繁琐且劳动密集，缺乏自动化工具来系统性地研究智能体行为。

Method: AEROBAT是一个多智能体系统，用户只需指定一个目标行为，系统即可自动执行完整的科学实验流程：生成关于该行为的假设、设计并执行受控实验、进行行为评估、分析实验结果并撰写研究报告。论文通过12个目标行为进行验证，共生成并测试79个假设，设计了1240个受控实验并执行了23512轮模拟。

Result: 在79个假设中有26个获得了中等至强的统计证据支持，其中包含一些新颖的发现。实验结果证明了自动化行为科学研究能够有效补充和扩展人工研究的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: AEROBAT证明了AI智能体的行为科学研究可以实现自动化，自动化的行为科学研究不仅能与人工研究互补，还能拓展人类研究的边界，为大规模、系统性地理解AI智能体行为提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [200] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)
*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHORUS是一个面向硬件验证场景的代码生成后训练框架，通过分阶段监督微调产生行为多样化的检查点，再利用密集奖励强化学习将其转化为各具任务特长的专家，最后通过模型合并或进一步后训练整合成单一4B模型，在CVDP-ECov上达到88.0%的Pass@1，超越DeepSeek-R1（671B）13.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件验证是现代芯片设计中占比巨大的关键环节，高覆盖率的测试台激励生成是核心任务。传统的SFT到RL的流水线已取得一定进展，但仍有进一步提升空间，且可执行反馈在代码生成中比纯文本模仿提供更可靠的学习信号。

Method: CHORUS包含两个核心观察和对应的技术路径：(1) 采用分阶段SFT（staged SFT）产生行为多样化的模型检查点，然后通过密集奖励RL将其训练成聚合性能相当但任务级别优势各异的专家；(2) 利用这些互补优势，通过训练免模型合并（training-free model merging）或进一步后训练来整合专家，最终将其固化为单一的4B模型。

Result: CHORUS在CVDP-ECov上达到88.0%的Pass@1，相比DeepSeek-R1（671B）提升了13.5个百分点，表明一个小得多的4B模型在硬件验证测试台生成任务上能够显著超越超大参数量的模型。

Conclusion: 通过利用分阶段SFT的行为多样性和密集奖励RL的专家化训练，再结合模型合并或后训练整合互补优势，可以在硬件验证代码生成任务上实现超越传统SFT-to-RL流水线和超大规模模型的效果，展示了后训练阶段系统化设计的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [201] [Evaluating Rational Contracting in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10475)
*Bhavyesh Sajja,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Roger Zimmermann,Tan Zhi-Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于语言智能体在不确定多步环境中协商和执行自然语言合同的理性框架，并构建了ContractSim评估套件。研究发现，当前LLM智能体在低不确定性下能达成高效合同，但在高不确定性下经常无法协商出令人满意、高效且双方互利的合同，并且在执行合同时经常违反条款以获取额外利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言的AI智能体扩展了机器经济活动的范围，但现有评估多聚焦于一次性交换或简单经济博弈，忽略了语言可表达的跨时间、有条件且不完备的丰富合同空间；同时现有评估只衡量原始利润，不衡量可信赖合同交易所需要的品质。因此需要一个理性框架来系统评估智能体的合同协商与执行能力。

Method: 论文首先形式化了一个理性框架，规定智能体应如何在不确定的多步环境中协商和执行自然语言合同。在此框架下开发了衡量理性与合作博弈的指标和基线，并将其实例化为ContractSim评估套件——一个由两位玩家在环境不确定性和玩家间不确定性下协商并执行多轮供应商合同的博弈环境。

Result: 在六个环境和三种供应商场景（餐饮、酒店清洁、AI托管）中，当前LLM智能体能够可靠地达成协议，且在环境不确定性低时能协商高效合同。然而在高不确定性下，它们经常无法协商出可满足的、高效的或互利的合同，并且在执行合同时经常不合作，即便合同容易满足也会为了额外利润违反合同条款。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在设计能够理性且合作地协商、解释和执行合同的语言智能体方面仍有很大改进空间，同时为这一领域提供了系统的评估框架和基准。

Abstract: The emergence of language-based AI agents promises to transform the scope of machine economic activity. Instead of just proposing bids or following hard-coded protocols, such agents can be used to negotiate and execute agreements in open-ended natural language. However, most evaluations of these abilities have focused on one-off exchanges or simple economic games, leaving open the rich space of time-extended, contingent, and incomplete contracts made expressible by language; they also focus on raw profit, without measuring the qualities required for trustworthy contracting. We address this by formulating a rational framework for how agents should negotiate and perform natural language contracts in uncertain multi-step environments. Within this framework, we develop metrics and baselines for quantifying rational and cooperative play. To evaluate how agents perform at such contracting, we instantiate our framework in ContractSim, an evaluation suite where two players negotiate and execute a multi-turn supplier contract under environmental and inter-player uncertainty. Across six environments and three supplier settings (catering, hotel cleaning, and AI hosting) we find that current LLM-based agents reach agreement reliably, and negotiate efficient contracts when environmental uncertainty is low. However, under high uncertainty, they often fail to negotiate satisfiable, efficient, or mutually beneficial contracts. They are also frequently uncooperative when executing contracts, violating contract terms for additional profit even when contracts are easy to satisfy. These findings highlight room for improvement in the design of language agents that can negotiate, interpret, and execute contracts both rationally and cooperatively.

</details>


### [202] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MESA框架，针对长时程智能体轨迹中的多结构记忆动态选择问题，通过端到端回答级弱监督学习，为每个查询自适应地选择和融合多个互补的结构化记忆视图，在AMA-Bench上比最强基线提升8.5%准确性，同时比全结构方案减少41%的证据token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的轨迹包含数百步交织的推理、行动和观察，回答问题可能需要依赖远处埋藏的证据。外部记忆将轨迹存储为结构化表示，但每种结构只提供不完整且不同的视图。现有多记忆系统要么为每个查询读取固定结构集（导致上下文膨胀和噪声引入），要么将查询路由到单一结构（无法组合互补证据）。受控分析表明，最优配置通常是多个结构记忆的定制化组合，随查询和任务需求变化。

Method: MESA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建五种互补的结构化视图来表示每条轨迹；(2) 使用端到端回答级反馈作为弱监督信号，从候选结构库中为每个查询选择并融合自适应的子集；(3) 采用harness优化，结合先验引导搜索（prior-guided search）和UCB引导调度（UCB-guided scheduling）来平衡探索与利用，以应对弱监督下的训练挑战。整个框架服务一个冻结的答案模型。

Result: 在AMA-Bench基准上，MESA相比最强基线在准确性上提升8.5%，同时相比使用全部结构的方案减少了41%的证据token数量，验证了查询自适应结构选择的有效性和效率优势。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结构级动态选择是比单一结构路由或全结构并读更优的范式。通过弱监督下的策略优化，MESA能够在保持回答质量的同时显著降低证据冗余，为长时程智能体的可扩展记忆管理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [203] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FiLLM框架，通过可执行计算树从真实表格中自动生成金融推理基准，无需人工标注即可大规模生成正确样本，并揭示LLM在金融表格推理中随深度和数值扰动性能大幅下降的问题，同时验证了LoRA微调能有效提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域评估LLM已有显著进展，但针对结构化数据上的金融推理任务探索不足，缺乏可扩展、无标注成本的可靠评估基准。

Method: 从真实表格构建可执行计算树，通过符号化求解获得ground truth，再将计算树渲染为自然语言问题，实现对深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文大小四个难度轴的独立可控调节；生成经过验证的思维链trace用于LoRA轻量微调。

Result: 开源模型在推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，在对抗性数值扰动下下降达47个百分点；LoRA微调将留出集准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，在FinQA上比基础模型高出5个百分点。

Conclusion: V-FiLLM提供了可扩展、无标注成本的金融推理基准生成框架，揭示了现有模型在深度推理和数值鲁棒性上的不足，同时表明低成本的LoRA微调是提升组合推理能力的有效方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [204] [VERDICT: Training-Free Step-Wise Verification of Multimodal Reasoning via Disagreement-Aware Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10665)
*Rohit Sinha,Kunal Tilaganji,Tanuja Ganu,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma,Vineeth Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VERDICT，一种无需训练的、跨领域自适应的逐步验证方法，通过形式化"不一致信息"为协调博弈问题，利用跨模态验证器间的分歧信号进行推理步骤验证，在六个基准上较基座模型最高提升+5.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的推理链中常含细微错误导致错误答案，现有验证方法存在两个关键局限：要么需要昂贵的标注监督且跨任务表现不一致，要么仅通过简单聚合多个来源的分数，忽略了分数间不一致本身携带的有效性判断信息。

Method: 将跨模态验证器间的评分协调形式化为一个协调博弈问题，推导出具有唯一闭式均衡解的耦合计分机制。基于此提出VERDICT方法：通过闭式解计算共识分数，实现不一致感知的过滤（disagreement-aware filtering）和稳定性感知的推理步骤排序（stability-conscious ranking）。该方法无需训练，不依赖特定领域。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，VERDICT较基座模型一致提升，最高提升+5.95%，并与需要大量监督的领域特定批评器性能相当，表明跨模态一致性信号无需任务特定适配即可提供稳健的验证信号。

Conclusion: 跨模态验证器间的一致性提供了无需训练、无需任务适配的稳健验证信号；分歧结构本身是可操作且可利用的有效信息，这为推理步骤验证提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Existing approaches either require expensive labelled supervision with inconsistent cross-task performance or aggregate scores from multiple sources by simple aggregations, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We formalise this as a coupled scoring problem among disparate, frozen verifiers, interpretable as a coordination game with a unique closed-form equilibrium where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Towards this end, we propose a training-free domain-agnostic step-wise verification approach we call VERDICT: VERification via Disagreement-Informed Coupled Thresholding. To our knowledge, VERDICT is the first training-free verifier that makes the structure of cross-modal disagreement explicit and actionable. It computes consensus scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, \method consistently improves over the base model by up to +5.95%, and performs competitively with domain-specific critics that demand extensive supervision, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation and Training-Free Verification

</details>


### [205] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI
